Ad-copy optimisation: scores and actual copy

Two blinded AI reviewers. Scores are editorial proxies, not conversion results. Visual specificity means picturable language; no images were generated.

Same three briefs: fair comparison

ConditionScore /100SolLuna
v1.1 generic62.057.3366.67
v1.1 playbook67.6759.3376.0
v1.1 playbook_cards63.1758.068.33
v1.2 playbook77.6773.6781.67
v1.2 playbook_cards79.6771.088.33

New experiment generation + reviews: $0.105472. No winning-ad claim. Material factual issues override numerical ranking.

Rubric and dimension scores
{
  "rubric": {
    "clarity": {
      "weight": 20,
      "good": "A shopper understands the product/message on first reading. Plain natural language, coherent headline and body.",
      "bad": "Jargon, tangled qualification, vague subject, or explanation of how to write instead of finished copy."
    },
    "visual_specificity": {
      "weight": 20,
      "good": "Concrete supported objects/actions or a plausible everyday moment the reader can picture. Visual means picturable writing, not evaluation of a generated image.",
      "bad": "Only an inventory list, abstract adjectives or an invented scene asserted as customer evidence."
    },
    "pain_relevance": {
      "weight": 15,
      "good": "Recognisable hesitation or practical frustration relevant to this brief, expressed empathetically without fear exploitation.",
      "bad": "Generic parenting reassurance, unrelated problem, exaggerated fear, or identical opening in all variants."
    },
    "benefit": {
      "weight": 20,
      "good": "Explains what the relevant supported feature changes for this shopper; a feature-led opening is allowed for informed shoppers.",
      "bad": "Lists contents/policies without explaining relevance, empty life transformation, or an unsupported outcome."
    },
    "crispness": {
      "weight": 15,
      "good": "Each sentence earns its place; direct natural wording, useful CTA, no filler or formulaic hype. Necessary qualifications remain concise.",
      "bad": "Repeated catalog lists, padded transitions, vague premium/ultimate/game-changing claims, redundant disclaimers or weak circular CTA."
    },
    "distinctiveness": {
      "weight": 10,
      "good": "The three variants offer genuinely different relevant angles or executions within the same facts and brief.",
      "bad": "Three paraphrases of the same product list or synonyms sold as new concepts."
    }
  },
